## Transcripts

### greet-zh

```
--- step 1: user says: 你好，你能帮我做什么？
  [partner message] 我是 Ducter，你的个人 AI 伙伴。比如你可以让我修好某个仓库里挂掉的测试。
```

### greet-en

```
--- step 1: user says: hi! what can you do?
  [partner message] I'm Ducter, your personal partner. For example, I can fix a failing test in one of your repos while you keep working.
```

### greet-ko

```
--- step 1: user says: 안녕하세요. 오늘 어떤 일을 도와줄 수 있나요?
  [partner message] 안녕하세요! 저는 Ducter예요. 이 컴퓨터에서 바로 할 수 있는 일을 도와드릴게요. 예를 들어 "어제 만든 보고서 파일 찾아줘"나 "내 메일에서 이번 주 일정 확인해줘" 같은 걸 부탁하시면 됩니다.
```

### ok-keeps-ko

```
--- step 1: user says: 고마워요. 내일 아침에 다시 얘기해요.
  [partner message] 안녕하세요, 내일 아침에 봐요.
--- step 2: user says: OK
  [partner message] 네, 내일 아침에 봐요.
```

### direct-ja

```
--- step 1: user says: 一言で：TCP と UDP の一番の違いは何ですか？
  [partner message] TCP は届いたことと順番を保証し、UDP は保証しない代わりに軽くて速いです。
```

### direct-es

```
--- step 1: user says: En dos líneas: ¿cuándo debo hacer rebase en vez de merge?
  [partner message] Usa rebase para actualizar tu rama privada con los cambios de la base antes de integrarla, así el historial queda lineal. Usa merge para integrar en ramas compartidas o públicas, porque nunca reescribe commits que otros ya tienen.
```

### direct-zh

```
--- step 1: user says: 一句话：TCP 和 UDP 最大的区别是什么？
  [partner message] TCP 是可靠的、有序的、面向连接的传输，先握手、丢了会重传；UDP 则是不保证送达和顺序的无连接传输，快但不管丢包。
```

### direct-en

```
--- step 1: user says: In two lines: when should I rebase instead of merge?
  [partner message] Rebase to tidy your own unshared branch before it goes up, so the history reads as one clean line. Merge when others already have the branch, or when you want the record to show exactly when the two lines of work came together.
```

### group-split-zh

```

### direct-zh-howto

```
--- step 1: user says: bun test 怎么只跑一个测试文件？
  [partner message] 用文件路径直接指给它就行：

```bash
bun test path/to/your.test.ts
```

想按测试名只跑其中几个，用 `-t`：

```bash
bun test -t "测试名的一部分"
```
```

Convert any imperial or other units found in the source files, and state each converted value's original only if the source is ambiguous. ⏎  ⏎ Done criteria: the doc exists in the bench folder; every number in it traces to a value in the source files (name the file for each table or key figure); no numbers are estimated or invented; if a figure is missing from the files, say it is missing rather than guessing. Report the file path you wrote and a one-line summary of the headline numbers. ⏎  ⏎ Out of scope: do not change benchmark code, configs
```

### mem-source-none-en

```
--- step 1: user says: In two sentences: what's a write-ahead log for?
  [partner message] A write-ahead log is an append-only file where a database records each change before applying it to the main data. If the system crashes, it replays that log on restart to recover committed changes, which keeps writes durable without rewriting the main data files on every change.
```

```
--- step 1: user says: 接着上周那个 compaction 的 PR，把 review 里说的锁粒度改细一点

  [sent to task compaction_pr] [user] 接着上周那个 compaction 的 PR，把 review 里说的锁粒度改细一点

要求：
- 按 review 里提到的锁粒度问题，把 compaction 过程中持有的锁范围缩小，只在真正需要的临界区内持锁。
- 不改动 compaction 的正确性语义；改完后相关测试要全部通过，并把测试输出作为证据报告。
- 在原分支上提交（不合并、不推送、不开新 PR，原来的 draft PR 保持不动）。
- 范围外：不做其他重构，不改无关模块。
- 完成后报告：改了哪些锁的范围、为什么这样改是安全的、测试结果。
```

(earlier) partner ack: 好，每天早上去看一次。  → task "每天看周五京沪高铁票价" working; its report: 第 3 天：周五 G1 553 元（二等座余 12 张），G3 553 元（余 4 张），G7 626 元；600 以下的有 2 班。
(earlier) partner: 周五 600 以下的还有两班：G1 和 G3，都是 553 元，G3 只剩 4 张。

--- step 1: user answers the open decision: go
  [partner message] 做好了，里面已经填上今天的 G1、G3 和 G7 票价，之后每天早上会跟着任务一起刷新。
  [an app card under the message: made "周五京沪高铁票价", with Open] the app's file now (v1, 1771 characters): <main class="k-app">
<section class="k-sum">
  <div class="k-sum-label">周五 北京→上海 · 600 元以下</div>
  <div class="k-sum-value" id="sum"><span class="k-none">还没看</span></div>
  <div class="k-sum-sub" id="sub">每天早上刷新</div>
</section>
<h2 class="k-h">班次（二等座）</h2>
<div class="k-card">
  <div class="k-list" id="list"></div>
</div>
<script>
function render(d){
  var sum = document.getElementById('sum');
  var sub = document.getElementById('sub');
  var list = document.getElementById('list');
  list.innerHTML = '';
  if (!d || !d.list) {
    sum.innerHTML = '<span class="k-none">还没看</span>';
    sub.textContent = '每天早上刷新';
    list.innerHTML = '<div class="k-empty">还没有班次</div>';
    return;
  }
  var t = d.threshold || 600;
  var under = d.list.filter(function(x){ return x.price <= t; });
  if (under.length) {
    var min = Math.min.apply(null, under.map(function(x){ return x.price; }));
    sum.textContent = '¥' + min + ' 起';
  } else {
    sum.innerHTML = '<span class="k-none">没有低于 ' + t + ' 的</span>';
  }
  sub.textContent = under.length + ' 班低于 ' + t + ' 元 · 更新于 ' + (d.updated || '—');
  d.list.forEach(function(x){
    var row = document.createElement('div');
    row.className = 'k-row';
    var seats = x.seats == null ? '余票未知' : '余 ' + x.seats + ' 张';
    var ok = x.price <= t;
    row.innerHTML =
      '<div class="k-row-main">' + x.no + '</div>' +
      '<div class="k-row-sub">' + seats + (ok ? '' : ' · 超过 ' + t) + '</div>' +
      '<div class="k-row-value" style="color:' + (ok ? 'var(--d-good)' : 'var(--d-muted)') + '">¥' + x.price + '</div>';
    list.appendChild(row);
  });
}
var got = ducter.get('trains');
Promise.resolve(got).then(render);
ducter.onChange(function(){ Promise.resolve(ducter.get('trains')).then(render); });
</script>
</main>; its data now (what it opens with): {"trains":{"threshold":600,"updated":"11-26 15:01","list":[{"no":"G1","price":553,"seats":12},{"no":"G3","price":553,"seats":4},{"no":"G7","price":626,"seats":null}]}}
  [Ducter passed the user's answer to task fares] [Ducter] The person now keeps this work's rounds in their small app "周五京沪高铁票价" (app a6e1d023b). Each round, write what stands now into it with app_data (app a6e1d023b, set {key: value}; null removes a key); the app reads the keys trains. Write whole values (a key's value is replaced), once a round. Then don't report_to_main for a round that only updated it: report only when something needs the per
  [also used tools] app
```

      list.innerHTML = '';
      empty.hidden = false;
      ducter.summary('No nights yet');
      return;
    }
    empty.hidden = true;
    var top = nights[0];
    latest.textContent = k(top.writes);
    sub.textContent = 'Night ' + top.n + ' · p99 ' + top.p99 + ' ms' +
      (top.stalls === undefined ? '' : ' · ' + top.stalls + ' compaction stalls');
    list.innerHTML = nights.map(function (r) {
      var detail = 'p99 ' + r.p99 + ' ms' +
        (r.stalls === undefined ? '' : ' · ' + r.stalls + ' compaction stalls');
      return '<div class="k-row"><div><div class="k-row-main">Night ' + esc(r.n) + '</div>' +
        '<div class="k-row-sub">' + esc(detail) + '</div></div>' +
        '<div class="k-row-value">' + esc(k(r.writes)) + '</div></div>';
    }).join('');
    ducter.summary('Night ' + top.n + ': ' + k(top.writes) + ', p99 ' + top.p99 + ' ms');
  }

  var first = ducter.get('nights');
  if (first && typeof first.then === 'function') first.then(render); else render(first);
  ducter.onChange(function () {
    var v = ducter.get('nights');
    if (v && typeof v.then === 'function') v.then(render); else render(v);
  });
})();

<div class="k-sum">
  <div class="k-sum-label">北京出差合计</div>
  <div class="k-sum-value" id="total">还没记</div>
  <div class="k-sum-sub" id="count"></div>
</div>
<div class="k-h">花销</div>
<div class="k-card"><div class="k-list" id="list"></div></div>
</main>
<script>
function draw(){
  const xs = ducter.get("expenses") || [];
  const list = document.getElementById("list");
  const total = document.getElementById("total");
  const count = document.getElementById("count");
  list.textContent = "";
  let sum = 0;
  for (const x of xs) {
    sum += Number(x.yuan) || 0;
    const row = document.createElement("div");
    row.className = "k-row";
    const main = document.createElement("div");
    main.className = "k-row-main";
    main.textContent = x.what;
    const sub = document.createElement("div");
    sub.className = "k-row-sub";
    sub.textContent = x.day;
    main.appendChild(sub);
    const val = document.createElement("div");
    val.className = "k-row-value";
    val.textContent = "¥" + (Number(x.yuan) || 0).toLocaleString("en-US");
    row.appendChild(main);
    row.appendChild(val);
    list.appendChild(row);
  }
  if (xs.length === 0) {
    const e = document.createElement("div");
    e.className = "k-empty";
    e.textContent = "还没有花销";
    list.appendChild(e);
    total.className = "k-sum-value k-none";
    total.textContent = "还没记";
    count.textContent = "";
  } else {
    total.className = "k-sum-value";
    total.textContent = "¥" + sum.toLocaleString("en-US");
    count.textContent = xs.length + " 笔";
  }
  ducter.summary(xs.length + " 笔,合计 ¥" + sum.toLocaleString("en-US"));
}
ducter.onChange(draw);
draw();
</script>; its data now (what it opens with): {"expenses":[{"what":"高铁","yuan":662,"day":"2026-11-18"},{"what":"酒店两晚","yuan":1036,"day":"2026-11-18"},{"what":"午饭","yuan":268,"day":"2026-11-19"}]}

var items = [];

function esc(s) {

  });
}
function fmt(n) {
  return '¥' + Number(n || 0).toLocaleString('en-US', { maximumFractionDigits: 2 });
}
function load() {
  items = ducter …  = i;
  renderEditor();
}
function saveEdit() {
  var amount = parseFloat(document.getElementById('e-amount').value);
  var item = document.getElementById('e-item').value.trim();
  if (!item || isNaN(amount)) return;
  items[editing] = {
    id: items[editing].id,
    item: item,
    date: document.getElementById('e-date').value.trim(),
    amount: amount,
    from: items[editing].from
  };
  editing = -1;
  persist();
  render();
}
function delEdit() {
  items.splice(editing, 1);
  editing = -1;
  persist();
  render();
}
document.getElementById('add').onclick = function () {
  var amount = parseFloat(document.getElementById('f-amount').value);
  var item = document.getElementById('f-item').value.trim();
  if (!item || isNaN(amount)) return;
  items.push({
    id: Date.now(),

    date: document.getElementById('f-date').value.trim(),
    amount: amount
  });
  document.getElementById('f-item').value = '';
  document.getElementById('f-date').value = '';
  document.getElementById('f-amount').value = '';
  document.querySelector('.k-add').removeAttribute('open');
  persist();
  render();
};
ducter.onChange(function () { load(); render(); });
load();
render();
</script>; its data now (what it opens with): {"expenses":[{"id":1,"date":"11/20","item":"高铁 G1 上海虹桥→北京南","amount":662,"from":"你的预订"},{"id":2,"date":"11/20–21","item":"亚朵酒店（望京）两晚","amount":1036,"from":"你的预订"}]}

```
(earlier) user: which of my PRs are still waiting on someone?
(earlier) partner: Four: wey-gu/ducter#412 (compaction cut) waits on Weizhen's review since Monday; nowledge-co/mem#10287 (retry on 502) on CI, failing; nowledge-co/hawdb#1592 (flaky compaction test) on Alice since Tuesday; wey-gu/ducter#418 (apps bar) is a draft.
--- step 1: user says: make that a little page I can keep and tick off as they land
  [partner message] Done: "PRs waiting on someone" is on your shelf with your four PRs, and you tap each row as it lands. It shows 4 still waiting now.
  [an app card under the message: made "PRs waiting on someone", with Open] the app's file now (v1, 2447 characters): <main class="k-app">
<div class="k-sum">
  <div class="k-sum-label">Still waiting</div>
  <div class="k-sum-value" id="n">4</div>
  <div class="k-sum-sub" id="s">tap a row when it lands</div>
</div>
<h2 class="k-h">My PRs</h2>
<div class="k-card"><ul class="k-list" id="list"></ul></div>
</main>
<style>
  .k-row { display:flex; align-items:center; gap:12px; cursor:pointer; }
  .k-row.done .k-row-main { text-decoration:line-through; color:var(--d-muted); }
  .k-row.done .k-row-value { color:var(--d-good); }
  .tick { width:28px; height:28px; border-radius:50%; border:2px solid var(--d-line); background:var(--d-card); color:var(--d-good); font-size:16px; line-height:24px; text-align:center; flex:none; padding:0; }
  .tick[aria-pressed="true"] { background:var(--d-good); border-color:var(--d-good); color:var(--d-card); }
  .k-row-main { flex:1; min-width:0; }
</style>
<script>
  var ROWS = ducter.get('prs') || [];
  function save() { ducter.set('prs', ROWS); }
  function render() {
    var list = document.getElementById('list');
    list.innerHTML = '';
    var open = 0;
    ROWS.forEach(function (r, i) {
      if (!r.done) open++;
      var li = document.createElement('li');
      li.className = 'k-row' + (r.done ? ' done' : '');
      var btn = document.createElement('button');
      btn.className = 'tick';
      btn.setAttribute('aria-pressed', r.done ? 'true' : 'false');
      btn.textContent = r.done ? '✓' : '';
      var main = document.createElement('div');
      main.className = 'k-row-main';
      var t = document.createElement('div');
      t.textContent = r.repo + ' ' + r.num + ' · ' + r.title;
      var sub = document.createElement('div');
      sub.className = 'k-row-sub';
      sub.textContent = r.waiting;
      main.appendChild(t);
      main.appendChild(sub);
      var val = document.createElement('div');
      val.className = 'k-row-value';
      val.textContent = r.done ? 'landed' : r.status;
      li.appendChild(btn);
      li.appendChild(main);
      li.appendChild(val);
      li.onclick = function () { ROWS[i].done = !ROWS[i].done; save(); render(); };
      list.appendChild(li);
    });
    document.getElementById('n').textContent = open;
    document.getElementById('s').textContent = open === 0 ? 'all landed' : 'tap a row when it lands';
    ducter.summary(open + ' still waiting');
  }
  ducter.onChange(function () { ROWS = ducter.get('prs') || ROWS; render(); });
  render();
</script>; its data now (what it opens with): {"prs":[{"repo":"wey-gu/ducter","num":"#412","title":"compaction cut","waiting":"waiting on Weizhen's review since Monday","status":"review","done":false},{"repo":"nowledge-co/mem","num":"#10287","title":"retry on 502","waiting":"on CI, failing","status":"CI","done":false},{"repo":"nowledge-co/hawdb","num":"#1592","title":"flaky compaction test","waiting":"waiting on Alice since Tuesday","status":"review","done":false},{"repo":"wey-gu/ducter","num":"#418","title":"apps bar","waiting":"still a draft","status":"draft","done":false}]}
